[86-11] Shailesh (Qualcomm)

DCI for V2V for email discussion to finalize the square-bracketed figure and FFS until Sept 2nd

Done: As per Mr Chair’s decision posted on Sept 3rd, the following is agreed:

DCI X for all BW

Let k be the number of sub-channels configured on the resource pool of a sub-carrier

CIF : 3 bits

Lowest index of sub-channel allocation ceil(log2(k)) = 0 to 5 bits

Time gap between tx and retx 4 bits

Frequency resource of initial and last tx ceil(log2(k*(k+1)/2) = 0 to 8 bits

If the number of information bits in V2V DCI format mapped onto a given search space is less than the payload size of format 0 mapped onto the same search space, zeros shall be appended to V2V DCI format until the payload size equals that of format 0 including any padding bits appended to format 0.

[86-12] Philippe (Huawei)

SCI contents for V2V

Email discussion until Sept 2nd to confirm the working assumption and square-bracketed figures, and the details of joint encoding of frequency location of the other transmission and number of subchannels

Done: As per Mr Chair’s decision posted on Sept 7th, the following is agreed:

Fixed size of 48 for the SCI

For frequency RA, copy the frequency RA field from DCI and pad it to 8 bits.

All other aspects (e.g., DMRS randomization) FFS to be addressed at next meeting including how to set the padding bits in frequency RA.

Attributes | Agreed values or assumptions | Further clarifications
Waveform | CP-OFDM as the UL waveformUL DMRS overhead, 1 OFDM symbol out of 7 OFDM symbols | Summary: no change to the existing agreements.
Resource allocation | A UE selects a MA physical resource randomly from a pool of orthogonal MA physical resourcesThere is no partial overlapping between the MA physical resources selected by more than one UEAll orthogonal MA physical resources are of same size | Total allocated bandwidth: 6RB, 4RB (optional) for calibration purpose only,Companies are encouraged to provide calibration results for both values,Either value can be used for later evaluation of the proposed MA scheme
 |  | Bandwidth per user per transmission: 1 RB
Receiver | MMSE, assuming realistic channel estimationNo blind decoding assumed | MMSE-IRC, assuming ideal channel estimation for calibration purpose only2RxNo blind decoding assumed
MCS | Same for all UesMCS is reported by each company | Same for all UEs,Derived by the bandwidth per user of 1 RB and TB size of 160 bits per transmissionQPSK is assumed
Power control | Open loop power controlAlpha=1, P0= -90 dBm | Summary: no change to the existing agreements. A few companies share different views. This power control setting for evaluation can be discussed later.
Packet size | Fixed by 20 bytes | TB size with CRC included
HARQ retransmission |  | No. of transmission is 1 (i.e., no repetition or retransmission)
Traffic model |  | FTP 3 with fixed TB size
Average no. of users per sector |  | 20 assuming 3 sectors/cell, total 57 sectors
Channel code |  | LTE Turbo
Any other potential parameters |  | Summary: how to present the results for calibration/comparison can be discussed later.

[86-22] Kazuaki (NTT DOCOMO)

Target packet drop rate for NR

Email discussion on the two options of the packet size and clarification on Target packet drop rate

as listed in R1-168510 until Sept. 1st

Done: According to Mr Chair’s email dated on Sept 3rd, the following clarifications are agreed:

Packet size

Observation : There is no consensus on which option to use. Each company would need to report the option used,. but are encouraged to evaluate both options. Decide on which option(s) is used to draw the conclusion of MA at RAN1#86bis meeting

Proposal 1

Option 1: Follow TR45.820

Option 2: Fix 40 Bytes

Target packet drop rate

Proposal 2: Packet drop rate = (Number of packet in outage) / (number of generated packets), where a packet is in outage if this packet failed to be successfully received by destination receiver beyond “Packet dropping timer”

Proposal 3: Companies report HARQ/retransmission assumption.

Proposal 4: Companies are encouraged to provide CDF of packet drop rate per UE.

[86-24] Wu Yong (Huawei)

Traffic model for NR for email discussion on traffic model for urban coverage for massive connection (UL) (until Sept 15th)

Done: According to Mr Chair’s email dated on Sept 29th, the following traffic model assumption is agreed for connection density evaluation, and not for coverage nor battery life evaluation.

Packet size

Follow proposal1 of [86-22] (proposal on packet size). Companies report which option(s) is used.

Packet arrival rate

Poisson packet arrival for each device, with arrival rate l.

Proponents are encouraged to report

What packet arrival rate (l) could be supported to achieve the connection density target, when packet arrival rate is assumed to be larger than what is assumed in TR45.820.

What connection density can be supported, when packet arrival rate is assumed the same as in TR45.820.
